<main class="wrap stories"><article class="story top" id="story-47522709" data-id="47522709" data-tags="Privacy|Security" data-score="900" data-comments="243" data-hn="https://news.ycombinator.com/item?id=47522709" data-time="1774470423">
<a class="thumb" href="https://fightchatcontrol.eu/?foo=bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#ec4899' data-tag='Privacy'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=fightchatcontrol.eu&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>fightchatcontrol.eu</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>
<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://fightchatcontrol.eu/?foo=bar" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The EU still wants to scan  your private messages and photos</span><span class='zh'>欧盟仍欲对私人信息与照片进行扫描</span></a><span class="dom">fightchatcontrol.eu</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Despite pushback, the EU continues to pursue scanning private messages and photos as part of its safety agenda.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟尽管遭到反对，仍在推动对私人信息与照片进行扫描，作为安全与反恐的一部分。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Despite pushback, the EU continues to pursue scanning private messages and photos as part of its safety agenda. The plan raises concerns about encryption, privacy, and the trade-offs between surveillance and individual rights.</span><span class='zh'>欧盟尽管遭到反对，仍在推动对私人信息与照片进行扫描，作为安全与反恐的一部分。该举动引发关于端对端加密、隐私保护与政府监控边界的担忧与争论。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">02</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://bugs.xdavidhu.me/tesla/2026/03/23/running-tesla-model-3s-computer-on-my-desk-using-parts-from-crashed-cars/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Running Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on my desk using parts from crashed cars</span><span class='zh'>用桌面裝置與回收車輛零件運行特斯拉 Model 3 的電腦</span></a><span class="dom">bugs.xdavidhu.me</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A maker demonstrates powering and running the Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on a desktop rig using salvaged parts from crashed cars.</span><span class='zh'>一位硬件爱好者用从撞车事故中回收的部件，在桌面上搭建并运行特斯拉 Model 3 的计算机。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A maker demonstrates powering and running the Tesla Model 3&#x27;s computer on a desktop rig using salvaged parts from crashed cars. The project shows automotive-grade compute can be repurposed for off-car experimentation, highlighting both learning value and safety/compatibility challenges.</span><span class='zh'>一位硬件爱好者用从撞车事故中回收的部件，在桌面上搭建并运行特斯拉 Model 3 的计算机。该项目展示了汽车级算力可以被改作桌面实验，但也暴露出安全与兼容性方面的挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Slowing down can protect long-term quality and prevent burnout in fast-moving tech work.</span><span class='zh'>放慢节奏或许比盲目追求速度更能保障长期产出质量，避免职业倦怠。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Slowing down can protect long-term quality and prevent burnout in fast-moving tech work. The piece argues for deliberate pacing, better boundaries, and smarter prioritization to sustain creativity and reliability over time.</span><span class='zh'>放慢节奏或许比盲目追求速度更能保障长期产出质量，避免职业倦怠。文中倡导有意识的节奏控制、设定边界以及更聪明的优先级排序，以维持创造力和可靠性。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">05</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/25/technology/social-media-trial-verdict.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta and YouTube found negligent in landmark social media addiction case</span><span class='zh'>Meta 与 YouTube 被认定在社媒成瘾案中存在过失——具有里程碑意义的裁决</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A landmark case finds Meta and YouTube negligent in contributing to social media addiction, with judges noting engagement-driven features that prioritize time-on-site over user well-being.</span><span class='zh'>在具里程碑意义的案件中，法院认定 Meta 与 YouTube 在促成社媒成瘾方面存在疏忽，法官指出以提升停留时间为目标的设计特征损害用户福祉。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A landmark case finds Meta and YouTube negligent in contributing to social media addiction, with judges noting engagement-driven features that prioritize time-on-site over user well-being. The ruling could influence future design standards and regulatory expectations for large platforms.</span><span class='zh'>在具里程碑意义的案件中，法院认定 Meta 与 YouTube 在促成社媒成瘾方面存在疏忽，法官指出以提升停留时间为目标的设计特征损害用户福祉。此判决可能影响未来对大型平台的设计标准与监管预期。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lapcatsoftware.com/articles/2026/3/11.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple randomly closes bug reports unless you &quot;verify&quot; the bug remains unfixed</span><span class='zh'>苹果在未验证问题仍未修复前随机关闭 bug 报告</span></a><span class="dom">lapcatsoftware.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple reportedly closes bug reports automatically if you don&#x27;t verify that the bug remains unfixed, complicating debugging workflows.</span><span class='zh'>据称苹果在未确认问题仍未修复的情况下，会随机关闭 bug 报告，使一些问题从活跃跟踪中消失，影响开发者的调试流程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple reportedly closes bug reports automatically if you don&#x27;t verify that the bug remains unfixed, complicating debugging workflows. The policy can cause issues to disappear from active tracking and hinder developers.</span><span class='zh'>据称苹果在未确认问题仍未修复的情况下，会随机关闭 bug 报告，使一些问题从活跃跟踪中消失，影响开发者的调试流程。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nature.com/articles/d41586-026-00950-w" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Antimatter has been transported for the first time</span><span class='zh'>反物质首次被运输</span></a><span class="dom">nature.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers have transported antimatter for the first time, marking a milestone in fundamental physics and enabling new tests of physical laws.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次实现了反物质运输，标志着基础物理学的里程碑，并为对物理定律的新检验提供可能。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers have transported antimatter for the first time, marking a milestone in fundamental physics and enabling new tests of physical laws. The achievement highlights advances in containment and manipulation of antimatter for future experiments.</span><span class='zh'>研究人员首次实现了反物质运输，标志着基础物理学的里程碑，并为对物理定律的新检验提供可能。此项突破凸显了在反物质的封存与操控方面的进步，未来或用于一系列实验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.latimes.com/business/story/2026-03-25/jury-says-meta-knowingly-harmed-children-for-profit-awarding-landmark-verdict" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Jury says Meta knowingly harmed children for profit, awarding landmark verdict</span><span class='zh'>陪审团认定 Meta 为盈利而知情伤害儿童，作出具有里程碑意义的判决</span></a><span class="dom">latimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A Los Angeles jury concluded Meta knowingly harmed children for profit, delivering a landmark verdict that could redefine platform liability.</span><span class='zh'>陪审团认定 Meta 为盈利而知情伤害儿童，作出具有里程碑意义的判决，或重新定义平台责任。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A Los Angeles jury concluded Meta knowingly harmed children for profit, delivering a landmark verdict that could redefine platform liability. The ruling intensifies scrutiny over social media design, addictive features, and the legal responsibilities of tech platforms toward minors.</span><span class='zh'>陪审团认定 Meta 为盈利而知情伤害儿童，作出具有里程碑意义的判决，或重新定义平台责任。此判决加剧对社交媒体设计、成瘾性功能以及科技平台对未成年用户的法律责任的审查。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/25/us/politics/supreme-court-cox-music-copyright.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Supreme Court Sides with Cox in Copyright Fight over Pirated Music</span><span class='zh'>最高法院在盗版音乐版权案中支持 Cox</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled in favor of Cox in a piracy-related music copyright dispute, overturning a lower-court decision.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院在涉及盗版音乐的版权纠纷中支持 Cox，推翻了下级法院的裁决。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Supreme Court ruled in favor of Cox in a piracy-related music copyright dispute, overturning a lower-court decision. The ruling narrows some safeguards around music rights enforcement and sets notable precedents for piracy cases.</span><span class='zh'>最高法院在涉及盗版音乐的版权纠纷中支持 Cox，推翻了下级法院的裁决。此判决对音乐版权执行的某些保护措施进行了收紧，并为盗版案件树立了重要判例。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">13</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.xda-developers.com/wine-11-rewrites-linux-runs-windows-games-speed-gains/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Wine 11 rewrites how Linux runs Windows games at kernel with massive speed gains</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11：在内核层重写以显著提升 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的速度</span></a><span class="dom">xda-developers.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Wine 11 overhauls kernel interactions to accelerate Windows games on Linux, delivering substantial speedups; it improves compatibility and reduces overhead in system calls translation and graphics handling. This move tightens the gap between Linux gaming and Windows performance, potentially expanding Linux gaming adoption.</span><span class='zh'>Wine 11 对内核层进行重写，使 Linux 上运行 Windows 游戏的性能显著提升，并提高了兼容性与系统调用翻译、图形处理的效率。此举缩小了 Linux 与 Windows 在游戏性能上的差距，有望推动 Linux 游戏生态的扩展。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ente.com/blog/ensu/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ensu – Ente’s Local LLM app</span><span class='zh'>Ensu — Ente 的本地大模型应用</span></a><span class="dom">ente.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ensu is Ente&#x27;s on-device LLM app that runs on-device, prioritizing privacy and offline use.</span><span class='zh'>Ensu 是 Ente 推出的本地大模型应用，支持在设备上离线运行，注重隐私与低延迟。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ensu is Ente&#x27;s on-device LLM app that runs on-device, prioritizing privacy and offline use. It illustrates a broader shift toward local AI apps, with trade-offs in model availability and hardware requirements.</span><span class='zh'>Ensu 是 Ente 推出的本地大模型应用，支持在设备上离线运行，注重隐私与低延迟。此类本地化 AI 应用反映了对数据控制的日益重视，但也面临模型获取与硬件条件的限制。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cql75dn07n2o" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Meta told to pay $375M for misleading users over child safety</span><span class='zh'>Meta 因误导儿童安全相关承诺被判赔偿 3.75 亿美元</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Regulators claim Meta misled users about child-safety commitments, resulting in a $375 million penalty.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构指控 Meta 在儿童安全承诺上误导用户，导致被处以 3.75 亿美元罚款。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Regulators claim Meta misled users about child-safety commitments, resulting in a $375 million penalty. The case underscores ongoing regulatory scrutiny of safety claims on large platforms and enforces accountability for how safety measures are communicated.</span><span class='zh'>监管机构指控 Meta 在儿童安全承诺上误导用户，导致被处以 3.75 亿美元罚款。此案凸显对大型平台在安全承诺与执行上的持续监管，以及对安全措施传播方式的问责。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://research.google/blog/turboquant-redefining-ai-efficiency-with-extreme-compression/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>TurboQuant: Redefining AI efficiency with extreme compression</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant：以极端压缩重塑AI效率</span></a><span class="dom">research.google</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>TurboQuant introduces extreme compression techniques to shrink AI models and cut compute needs while preserving accuracy. This approach tests the boundaries of model distillation and quantization, potentially slashing deployment costs and enabling more edge and on-device inference. Trade-offs remain: training compatibility and potential accuracy quirks in edge cases.</span><span class='zh'>TurboQuant 引入极端压缩技术，在保持准确度的同时显著降低模型规模和计算需求，有望降低大规模部署成本并推动边缘设备或端侧推理的普及。该方法可能带来训练兼容性与在极端情形下的精度波动等权衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.jakesaunders.dev/is-anybody-else-bored-of-talking-about-ai/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Is anybody else bored of talking about AI?</span><span class='zh'>还有人觉得聊 AI 吗？</span></a><span class="dom">blog.jakesaunders.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective essay questions whether the constant AI chatter has worn people down and argues for focusing on practical, real-world impact over hype. It pushes readers to value concrete use cases, governance, and measurable progress rather than trending headlines.</span><span class='zh'>这篇随笔质疑持续的 AI 热议是否让人疲惫，并主张把重点放在现实世界的实际影响而非炒作。作者鼓励关注具体的使用场景、治理与可衡量的进展，而不是追逐热度新闻。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.sambent.com/microsofts-plan-to-fix-windows-11-is-gaslighting/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microsoft&#x27;s &quot;fix&quot; for Windows 11</span><span class='zh'>微软对 Windows 11 的“修复”：挨打后的花束</span></a><span class="dom">sambent.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Microsoft continues to roll out patches for Windows 11, but critics argue the changes are cosmetic and don&#x27;t address core reliability or usability issues. The messaging resembles a post-incident PR sprint rather than substantive engineering fixes. For tech readers, this highlights the gap between marketing narratives and real-world OS quality in major updates.</span><span class='zh'>微软继续推出 Windows 11 的补丁，但批评者认为这些改动多为表面修补，无法解决核心的可靠性或可用性问题。外部宣传更像是事后公关冲刺，而非实质工程改进。对技术读者而言，这凸显了大型操作系统更新中营销叙述与实际软件质量之间的差距。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tell HN: Litellm 1.82.7 and 1.82.8 on PyPI are compromised</span><span class='zh'>Litellm 1.82.7 与 1.82.8 在 PyPI 上遭篡改</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Two releases of a lightweight LLM client package were compromised on PyPI, enabling malicious code pushes to downstream users. The incident underscores supply-chain risk in Python ecosystems and the importance of checksum validation and pinning versions. Users should audit dependencies, upgrade to clean builds, and verify package integrity.</span><span class='zh'>两个版本的轻量级 LLM 客户端包在 PyPI 的发布被篡改，导致后续用户可能被注入恶意代码。事件凸显 Python 生态的供应链风险，强调校验和版本锁定的重要性。请审计依赖、升级到干净的构建版本，并验证包的完整性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.apple.com/newsroom/2026/03/introducing-apple-business-a-new-all-in-one-platform-for-businesses-of-all-sizes/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Apple Business</span><span class='zh'>Apple Business：面向各规模企业的一体化平台</span></a><span class="dom">apple.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Apple introduces Apple Business, an all-in-one platform to streamline device management, security, and app deployment for organizations of all sizes. By consolidating IT controls and enterprise workflows, it aims to reduce friction for Apple-powered ecosystems in the workplace. The move could reshape enterprise IT purchasing and cross-device collaboration strategies.</span><span class='zh'>苹果推出 Apple Business，一站式平台用于简化企业级设备管理、安全性与应用部署，覆盖各类规模的组织。通过整合 IT 控制和企业工作流，意在降低在工作场景中使用苹果设备的门槛。这一举措可能影响企业 IT 的采购决策及跨设备协作方式。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://videojs.org/blog/videojs-v10-beta-hello-world-again" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I took back Video.js after 16 years and we rewrote it to be 88% smaller</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：16 年后重新拿回 Video.js，并将其重写使体积缩小 88%</span></a><span class="dom">videojs.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN: after reclaiming control of Video.js, the team rebuilt the project to a leaner 12% of its former size, maintaining compatibility while shaving bandwidth and load time.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：重新掌控 Video.js 后，团队将其重写，使体积仅为原来的 12%，并保持向后兼容，同时减小带宽和加载时间。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN: after reclaiming control of Video.js, the team rebuilt the project to a leaner 12% of its former size, maintaining compatibility while shaving bandwidth and load time. The rewrite by the maintainers highlights how modern tooling and modular design can yield dramatic size reductions.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：重新掌控 Video.js 后，团队将其重写，使体积仅为原来的 12%，并保持向后兼容，同时减小带宽和加载时间。此重写展示了现代工具链与模块化设计在实现显著体积缩减方面的潜力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/BerriAI/litellm/issues/24512" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Malicious litellm_init.pth in litellm 1.82.8 PyPI package – credential stealer</span><span class='zh'>litellm 1.82.8 PyPI 包中的恶意 litellm_init.pth：凭据窃取</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A malicious PyPI package for litellm 1.82.8 contains a litellm_init.pth file that loads a credential-stealing module during import. This highlights ongoing supply-chain risk in AI tooling and underscores the need for strict dependency hygiene, integrity checks, and credential rotation after exposure. Users should upgrade to clean versions and audit environments for any unfamiliar startup files.</span><span class='zh'>在 litellm 1.82.8 的 PyPI 包中发现恶意的 litellm_init.pth，导入时会加载窃取凭据的模块。此事件暴露了 AI 工具链中的供应链风险，并强调对依赖项进行严格校验、完整性检查以及暴露后轮换凭据的必要性。如有怀疑，应升级到干净版本并审计运行环境中是否存在异常的启动文件。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://v-os.dev" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS – Desktop Linux Inspired by the BeOS</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS：受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">v-os.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS is a desktop Linux distribution inspired by BeOS, aiming for a fast, responsive UI and streamlined media workflows.</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS 是一个受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux 发行版，着重实现快速、响应式的界面与流畅的多媒体工作流。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>VitruvianOS is a desktop Linux distribution inspired by BeOS, aiming for a fast, responsive UI and streamlined media workflows. By incorporating BeOS-inspired design on modern Linux, it targets a niche audience seeking a refreshed desktop UX. Adoption will depend on hardware support and ecosystem maturity.</span><span class='zh'>VitruvianOS 是一个受 BeOS 启发的桌面 Linux 发行版，着重实现快速、响应式的界面与流畅的多媒体工作流。它在现代 Linux 上复兴 BeOS 式的用户体验，面向寻求新鲜桌面体验的用户群体。普及程度将取决于对硬件的支持和生态系统的成熟度。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.onhand.pro/p/i-wanted-to-build-vertical-saas-for-pest-control-i-took-a-technician-job-instead" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I wanted to build vertical SaaS for pest control, so I took a technician job</span><span class='zh'>我想为害虫防治做垂直 SaaS，结果去做了现场技师工作</span></a><span class="dom">onhand.pro</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>I aimed to build a vertical SaaS for pest control, but I took a technician job to learn the field from the ground up.</span><span class='zh'>我原本计划为害虫防治领域开发垂直型 SaaS，但亲自去做了一线技师。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>I aimed to build a vertical SaaS for pest control, but I took a technician job to learn the field from the ground up. The experience surfaced practical constraints—on-site workflows, regulatory checks, and the value of real-time field data—that aren’t obvious from dashboards alone. The takeaway is that deep domain immersion often makes or breaks vertical SaaS success.</span><span class='zh'>我原本计划为害虫防治领域开发垂直型 SaaS，但亲自去做了一线技师。现场工作揭示了实际工作流、合规检查和需实时采集的现场数据等难题，这些在只看仪表盘时往往看不到。关键 takeaway 是，深入领域实操常常决定垂直 SaaS 成功与否。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.bbc.com/news/articles/cg547ljepvzo" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Mystery jump in oil trading ahead of Trump post draws scrutiny</span><span class='zh'>特朗普发帖前油价交易出现神秘跳涨，引发关注</span></a><span class="dom">bbc.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Crude oil markets showed an unexplained rally ahead of a high-profile Trump post, drawing scrutiny from traders and regulators alike. Analysts debate whether rapid algorithmic trading, order-flow dynamics, or potential insider information drove the move. The episode underscores how political events can instantly ripple through commodity markets.</span><span class='zh'>在特朗普发布重要言论前，原油市场出现难以解释的跳涨，吸引交易员和监管机构的关注。分析师讨论是否因快速的算法交易、订单流动性变化，或内部信息导致了这一下跳。此事凸显政治事件能即时波及大宗商品市场。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/data-center-dc" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Data centers are transitioning from AC to DC</span><span class='zh'>爱迪生复仇：数据中心正从交流电向直流电过渡</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Data centers are increasingly adopting DC power distribution to reduce losses, improve efficiency, and simplify cabling.</span><span class='zh'>数据中心正越来越多地采用直流供电分配，以降低能耗、提升效率并简化布线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Data centers are increasingly adopting DC power distribution to reduce losses, improve efficiency, and simplify cabling. The trend echoes Edison-era ideas of direct power delivery and could influence rack-level design and energy management strategies.</span><span class='zh'>数据中心正越来越多地采用直流供电分配，以降低能耗、提升效率并简化布线。这一趋势回响了直流供电的理念，可能影响机架级设计与能源管理策略。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://newsroom.arm.com/blog/introducing-arm-agi-cpu" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Arm AGI CPU</span><span class='zh'>Arm AGI CPU：面向通用人工智能的新一代处理器</span></a><span class="dom">newsroom.arm.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Arm unveils an AGI-oriented CPU design aimed at accelerating general AI workloads across edge and data-center scale. The architecture focuses on high-bandwidth memory, advanced vector math, and security features to support large models with lower power. This signals Arm&#x27;s push to shape the AI compute ecosystem beyond accelerators, integrating AI-friendly cores into mainstream chips.</span><span class='zh'>Arm 面向通用人工智能推出新一代 CPU 设计，意在在边缘和数据中心规模上加速通用 AI 工作负载。该架构强调高带宽内存、高级向量运算以及安全特性，以在更低功耗下支撑大模型。此举标志着 Arm 正在推动 AI 计算生态，不仅依赖加速器，也在主流芯片中整合对 AI 友好的核心。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.answer.ai/posts/2026-03-12-so-where-are-all-the-ai-apps.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>So where are all the AI apps?</span><span class='zh'>AI 应用到底在哪儿？</span></a><span class="dom">answer.ai</span></h3>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/ssrajadh/sentrysearch" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Gemini can now natively embed video, so I built sub-second video search</span><span class='zh'>Gemini 现可原生嵌入视频，我实现了亚秒级视频搜索</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gemini now supports native video embedding, enabling richer media integration in AI workflows. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.emailmd.dev/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Email.md – Markdown to responsive, email-safe HTML</span><span class='zh'>Email.md：将 Markdown 转换为响应式、邮件安全的 HTML</span></a><span class="dom">emailmd.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Email.md provides a conversion tool turning Markdown into HTML that renders consistently across email clients.</span><span class='zh'>Email.md 提供一种将 Markdown 转换为在各大邮件客户端中呈现一致的 HTML 的工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Email.md provides a conversion tool turning Markdown into HTML that renders consistently across email clients. It tackles email-inbox quirks like inlined styles and tables for layout, enabling safer, more reliable newsletters and transactional messages. For developers, it&#x27;s a time-saver when shipping marketing emails.</span><span class='zh'>Email.md 提供一种将 Markdown 转换为在各大邮件客户端中呈现一致的 HTML 的工具。它解决了邮件客户端对样式内联、表格布局等的限制造成的问题，从而让新闻简报与交易邮件变得更安全、可靠。对开发者来说，是发布邮件内容时的省时工具。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://smu160.github.io/posts/missile-defense-is-np-complete/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Missile defense is NP-complete</span><span class='zh'>导弹防御是 NP 完全问题</span></a><span class="dom">smu160.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post argues that certain missile defense planning problems are NP-complete, implying intrinsic computational limits for optimal real-time solutions. The takeaway is that heuristics and approximations remain essential in defense system design.</span><span class='zh'>文中指出某些导弹防御规划问题属于 NP 完全，暗示在实时求解中存在本质的计算极限。结论是，在防御系统设计中仍需依赖启发式与近似方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/anemll/status/2035901335984611412" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>iPhone 17 Pro Demonstrated Running a 400B LLM</span><span class='zh'>iPhone 17 Pro 展示本地运行 400B 参数的 LLM</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An iPhone 17 Pro demonstration shows a 400-billion-parameter LLM running locally on-device, signaling advances in edge AI and privacy-preserving inference. The feat highlights model compression, efficient on-device runtimes, and the potential shift away from cloud-only AI.</span><span class='zh'>一段演示显示 iPhone 17 Pro 能在本地设备上运行 400B 参数的 LLM，标志边缘 AI 的进展与隐私保护推理能力。此成就凸显模型压缩、边缘运行效率的提升，以及 AI 由云端依赖向本地推理的潜在转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://antithesis.com/blog/2026/hegel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hypothesis, Antithesis, synthesis</span><span class='zh'>Hypothesis、对立、综合</span></a><span class="dom">antithesis.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece revisits Hegelian dialectics, framing problem-solving as a dynamic progression from thesis to antithesis and ultimately synthesis.</span><span class='zh'>本文重新梳理黑格尔辩证法，将问题解决描述为从假设到对立再到综合的动态过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece revisits Hegelian dialectics, framing problem-solving as a dynamic progression from thesis to antithesis and ultimately synthesis. It argues that embracing tension between ideas can drive deeper solutions in tech design, policy, and philosophy rather than chasing binary outcomes. The takeaway is to cultivate iterative refinement rather than one-off conclusions.</span><span class='zh'>本文重新梳理黑格尔辩证法，将问题解决描述为从假设到对立再到综合的动态过程。它主张在技术设计、政策制定与哲学领域拥抱思想张力，避免简单二元对立，以推动更深层次的解决方案。要点是强调迭代完善而非一次性结论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling.</span><span class='zh'>2016 年的比较显示 Ripgrep 在大型代码库中明显快于 grep、ag、git grep 等工具，得益于 Rust 实现和更高效的正则处理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A 2016 comparison shows Ripgrep outperforms traditional search tools on large codebases thanks to Rust-based internals and smarter regex handling. It helped popularize fast, developer-friendly search in modern toolchains.</span><span class='zh'>2016 年的比较显示 Ripgrep 在大型代码库中明显快于 grep、ag、git grep 等工具，得益于 Rust 实现和更高效的正则处理。这推动了现代工具链对快速搜索的追求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://danielhomola.com/m%20&amp;%20e/ai/your-bridge-to-wealth-is-being-pulled-up/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The bridge to wealth is being pulled up with AI</span><span class='zh'>AI抬高了致富之路的门槛</span></a><span class="dom">danielhomola.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI is reshaping how people build wealth by enabling new models of automation, entrepreneurship, and platform-enabled investing.</span><span class='zh'>AI正在重塑致富路径，通过自动化、创业和平台化投资等新模式降低财富门槛，同时也在重塑传统职业阶梯。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI is reshaping how people build wealth by enabling new models of automation, entrepreneurship, and platform-enabled investing. While AI lowers barriers to value creation, it also reorders traditional career ladders, pushing many toward AI-enabled ventures and ownership rather than straight wages.</span><span class='zh'>AI正在重塑致富路径，通过自动化、创业和平台化投资等新模式降低财富门槛，同时也在重塑传统职业阶梯。AI可能让人更易创造价值，但也促使不少人从单纯靠工资的工作转向基于AI的创业和资产所有权。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.fcc.gov/document/fcc-updates-covered-list-include-foreign-made-consumer-routers" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>FCC updates covered list to include foreign-made consumer routers</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单</span></a><span class="dom">fcc.gov</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The FCC expands the Covered Equipment list to include foreign-made consumer routers, tightening security requirements for home networking gear sold in the U.S. The move could affect manufacturers, supply chains, and consumer privacy as compliance costs become clearer.</span><span class='zh'>FCC 将外国制造的消费路由器纳入覆盖清单，强化对美国市场网络设备的安全要求。此举可能影响厂商、供应链与合规成本，进而关系到家庭网络的隐私与安全。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://lnav.org/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Log File Viewer for the Terminal</span><span class='zh'>终端日志查看器</span></a><span class="dom">lnav.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>lnav is a powerful terminal-based log viewer that aggregates and parses multiple log formats, offering fast search, color highlighting, and time-based filtering.</span><span class='zh'>lnav 是一个强大的终端日志查看工具，能聚合并解析多种日志格式，支持快速搜索、颜色高亮和基于时间的过滤。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>lnav is a powerful terminal-based log viewer that aggregates and parses multiple log formats, offering fast search, color highlighting, and time-based filtering. It makes triaging issues across services easier by providing structured views and cross-reference across files without leaving the terminal.</span><span class='zh'>lnav 是一个强大的终端日志查看工具，能聚合并解析多种日志格式，支持快速搜索、颜色高亮和基于时间的过滤。它通过在同一界面中跨文件浏览和关联信息，简化了跨服务的问题排查与调试。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A discreet retrofit of a standalone apartment intercom to integrate with Apple HomeKit enables automations and remote control without replacing the entire system.</span><span class='zh'>本文记录了如何低调为公寓对讲机做改造，使其能接入 Apple HomeKit，从而在家庭自动化中实现通过 Home.app 的控制与场景触发。强调在利用现有设备构建生态时需要关注安全、稳定性以及对硬件的潜在风险和保修影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The BlackRock CEO warns that oil at $150 triggers a global recession, highlighting the macro risk of energy shocks to growth, inflation, and monetary policy.</span><span class='zh'>黑石集团首席执行官警告称，油价若升至150美元可能引发全球衰退，凸显能源冲击对增长、通胀和货币政策的宏观风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The BlackRock CEO warns that oil at $150 triggers a global recession, highlighting the macro risk of energy shocks to growth, inflation, and monetary policy. The comment adds to a chorus of voices stressing energy price sensitivity and resilience planning for investors.</span><span class='zh'>黑石集团首席执行官警告称，油价若升至150美元可能引发全球衰退，凸显能源冲击对增长、通胀和货币政策的宏观风险。此言论加剧了对能源价格敏感性及投资者韧性规划的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/t8/hypura" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Hypura – A storage-tier-aware LLM inference scheduler for Apple Silicon</span><span class='zh'>Hypura：面向 Apple Silicon 的存储层感知 LLM 推理调度器</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware scheduler that optimizes LLM inference on Apple Silicon by factoring in where data lives (fast cache vs slower storage).</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入了一种面向存储层感知的推理调度器，在 Apple Silicon 上通过考虑数据所在的存储层次（高速缓存与较慢存储）来优化 LLM 推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Hypura introduces a storage-tier-aware scheduler that optimizes LLM inference on Apple Silicon by factoring in where data lives (fast cache vs slower storage). By aligning memory, compute, and I/O, it can reduce latency and boost throughput for on-device AI workloads. This approach highlights how storage topology awareness can unlock more efficient AI on consumer hardware.</span><span class='zh'>Hypura 引入了一种面向存储层感知的推理调度器，在 Apple Silicon 上通过考虑数据所在的存储层次（高速缓存与较慢存储）来优化 LLM 推理。通过对齐内存、计算和 I/O，可以降低延迟、提升本地 AI 的吞吐量。这种做法凸显了存储拓扑感知在消费级硬件上提升 AI 效率的潜力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Disney ended its OpenAI deal after OpenAI shut down Sora, a video AI app.</span><span class='zh'>迪士尼在 OpenAI 交易中退出 Sora 项目，标志着合作关系的脆弱性以及产品生命周期对战略决策的影响。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Disney ended its OpenAI deal after OpenAI shut down Sora, a video AI app. The move illustrates how strategic partnerships hinge on product viability and the fragility of AI vendor ecosystems when a flagship tool is discontinued.</span><span class='zh'>迪士尼在 OpenAI 交易中退出 Sora 项目，标志着合作关系的脆弱性以及产品生命周期对战略决策的影响。该举动反映出在 AI 生态中，关键工具的可用性直接左右伙伴关系的走向。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>New details reveal SKUs and specifications for the ARM AGI CPU, outlining core counts, cache structure, memory bandwidth, and possible ISA extensions tailored for AI workloads.</span><span class='zh'>ARM AGI CPU 的规格与型号清单曝光，涵盖核心数量、缓存结构、内存带宽，以及面向 AI 工作负载的 ISA 扩展等要点。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>New details reveal SKUs and specifications for the ARM AGI CPU, outlining core counts, cache structure, memory bandwidth, and possible ISA extensions tailored for AI workloads. The lineup signals how Arm plans to balance performance, power, and software compatibility as AGI tasks scale across devices. For developers and OEMs, the specs map to anticipated optimization opportunities and ecosystem tooling.</span><span class='zh'>ARM AGI CPU 的规格与型号清单曝光，涵盖核心数量、缓存结构、内存带宽，以及面向 AI 工作负载的 ISA 扩展等要点。该系列展示了 Arm 如何在 AI 任务大规模化时平衡性能、功耗与软件兼容性，便于开发者和原始设备制造商把握优化机会与生态工具链。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.wheresyoured.at/the-ai-industry-is-lying-to-you/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The AI Industry Is Lying to You</span><span class='zh'>AI 行业在欺骗你</span></a><span class="dom">wheresyoured.at</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that hype around AI overstates capabilities and obscures risks, urging skepticism and demand for transparency, governance, and responsible deployment.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为对 AI 的宣传过于夸大能力、掩盖风险，呼吁保持怀疑态度、提升透明度、加强治理，并在部署时负责任地权衡。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An opinion piece argues that hype around AI overstates capabilities and obscures risks, urging skepticism and demand for transparency, governance, and responsible deployment. It questions the marketing of AGI as imminent and calls for nuance in interpreting model performance and limitations.</span><span class='zh'>一篇观点文章认为对 AI 的宣传过于夸大能力、掩盖风险，呼吁保持怀疑态度、提升透明度、加强治理，并在部署时负责任地权衡。文章质疑将通用人工智能的实现过度渲染为近在眼前，并强调对模型性能与局限性的审慎解读。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A country reportedly embedded backdoors in Cisco routers to facilitate surveillance and has banned foreign-made routers.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称某国在思科路由器中植入后门以便进行监控，现禁止进口外国路由器。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A country reportedly embedded backdoors in Cisco routers to facilitate surveillance and has banned foreign-made routers. The incident highlights geopolitical risk in critical infrastructure supply chains and underscores the need for hardened, trusted hardware and diversified sourcing.</span><span class='zh'>有报道称某国在思科路由器中植入后门以便进行监控，现禁止进口外国路由器。这一事件凸显关键基础设施供应链的地缘政治风险，并强调对更可信任的硬件与多元采购的需求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wolfssl/wolfguard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>WolfGuard: WireGuard with FIPS 140-3 cryptography</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard：带 FIPS 140‑3 加密的 WireGuard</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>WolfGuard is a WireGuard-focused project that implements FIPS 140-3 cryptography, aligning VPN tech with government-grade security standards. This makes it appealing for regulated industries or sensitive deployments, though it may come with certification overhead and potential compatibility considerations with standard WireGuard deployments.</span><span class='zh'>WolfGuard 是一个聚焦于 WireGuard 的项目，实现了 FIPS 140-3 加密，确保 VPN 技术符合政府等级的安全标准。对合规行业或敏感部署具有吸引力，但可能伴随认证成本和与常规 WireGuard 部署的兼容性挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GitHub appears to be facing reliability issues despite being a core platform for developers, raising concerns about SLOs and uptime for massive code and CI pipelines. The outages stress the need for stronger resilience planning and incident response.</span><span class='zh'>GitHub 作为开发者的核心平台，正面临可靠性问题，令大规模代码托管与持续集成流水线的可用性成为焦点。事件暴露出在弹性设计和应急响应方面的不足，强调需要更强的容错能力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/AmElmo/proofshot" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: ProofShot – Give AI coding agents eyes to verify the UI they build</span><span class='zh'>Show HN: ProofShot – 让 AI 编码代理具备验证 UI 的能力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ProofShot enables AI coding agents to inspect and verify the UI they generate, acting as a visual verifier for automated UI construction. This reduces misalignment between intended design and rendered output, accelerating trusted AI-driven UI workflows. It showcases a practical approach to injecting human-in-the-loop feedback into automated UI pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>ProofShot 让 AI 编码代理能够检查并验证它们生成的 UI，作为对自动化 UI 构建的可视化校验工具。它减少了设计意图与最终呈现之间的错配，加速了 AI 驱动的 UI 工作流程中的信任建立。展示了将人类在环反馈引入自动化 UI 流程的实际方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/localstack/localstack" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Local Stack Archived their GitHub repo and requires an account to run</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 将仓库归档，运行需账户</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>LocalStack has archived its GitHub repository and now requires you to sign in to run the software locally. This marks a shift from open development toward a gated model, potentially affecting developers who rely on a free, self-hosted AWS-like environment. The move invites questions about accessibility and sustainability of community-focused open-source projects.</span><span class='zh'>LocalStack 已将其 GitHub 仓库归档，现在运行需要账户登录。这标志着从开放开发向受限访问的转变，可能影响依赖免费本地 AWS 模拟环境的开发者。此举也引发关于社区开源项目可访问性与可持续性的讨论。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://blog.ydb.tech/how-io-uring-overtook-libaio-performance-across-linux-kernels-and-an-unexpected-iommu-trap-ea6126d9ef14" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>io_uring, libaio performance across Linux kernels and an unexpected IOMMU trap</span><span class='zh'>io_uring、libaio 在不同 Linux 内核的性能对比与意外的 IOMMU 陷阱</span></a><span class="dom">blog.ydb.tech</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The post analyzes performance of io_uring vs libaio across Linux kernel versions, noting improvements and a surprising IOMMU trap that affects certain workloads.</span><span class='zh'>文章比较了 io_uring 与 libaio 在不同 Linux 内核版本中的性能，指出性能改进同时暴露出某些工作负载会遇到的意外 IOMMU 陷阱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The post analyzes performance of io_uring vs libaio across Linux kernel versions, noting improvements and a surprising IOMMU trap that affects certain workloads. It underscores evolving I/O APIs trade-offs and the importance of kernel version choice for latency-sensitive apps, storage stacks, and virtualization.</span><span class='zh'>文章比较了 io_uring 与 libaio 在不同 Linux 内核版本中的性能，指出性能改进同时暴露出某些工作负载会遇到的意外 IOMMU 陷阱。强调了 I/O API 的演变及对延迟敏感应用、存储栈和虚拟化的内核版本选择的重要性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/TechEmpower/FrameworkBenchmarks/issues/10932" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Sunsetting the Techempower Framework Benchmarks</span><span class='zh'>停用 Techempower 框架基准</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Techempower Framework Benchmarks project is being sunset, ending active maintenance and updates. The move reflects the ongoing maintenance burden and evolving ecosystems, nudging developers to rely on maintained benchmarks or run their own tests while retaining historical performance insights.</span><span class='zh'>Techempower 框架基准项目将进入停止维护阶段，正式结束主动更新。此举反映出维护负担与生态变化的压力，促使开发者改为使用受维护的基准集或自行执行性能测试，同时保留历史性能快照作为参考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://socket.dev/blog/trivy-under-attack-again-github-actions-compromise" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Trivy under attack again: Widespread GitHub Actions tag compromise secrets</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再度受攻击：GitHub Actions 标签广泛暴露密钥</span></a><span class="dom">socket.dev</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Trivy is again under fire after attackers exploited compromised GitHub Actions tags to steal secrets.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再次受攻击，攻击者利用被妥协的 GitHub Actions 标签窃取密钥。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Trivy is again under fire after attackers exploited compromised GitHub Actions tags to steal secrets. The incident underscores how software supply chain tools can be weaponized through CI/CD workflows, prompting teams to pin trusted versions, audit action usage, and rotate leaked credentials.</span><span class='zh'>Trivy 再次受攻击，攻击者利用被妥协的 GitHub Actions 标签窃取密钥。这凸显供应链工具在 CI/CD 流水线中的潜在攻击点，促使团队固定可信版本、审计 Actions 使用并轮换泄露的凭证。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/dune3d/dune3d" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Dune3d: A parametric 3D CAD application</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d：一个参数化的3D CAD应用</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dune3d is an open-source parametric 3D CAD project that lets users define and modify geometry with parameters and constraints. The project exemplifies how modular, scriptable CAD tooling could complement or challenge heavyweight CAD suites.</span><span class='zh'>Dune3d 是一个开源的参数化3D CAD项目，允许通过参数和约束来定义和修改几何形状。该项目展示了轻量、可脚本化的 CAD 工具如何挑战主流工具并实现更快速的试验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://searchengineland.com/walmart-chatgpt-checkout-converted-worse-472071" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Walmart: ChatGPT checkout converted 3x worse than website</span><span class='zh'>沃尔玛：ChatGPT 结账转化率比官网低三倍</span></a><span class="dom">searchengineland.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A test of ChatGPT-powered checkout at a major retailer shows conversions were three times worse than the regular checkout flow. The result underscores that conversational AI must be carefully designed for shopping tasks to avoid frictions, even as AI promises convenience.</span><span class='zh'>在一家大型零售商的结账场景中，ChatGPT 驱动的结账流程的转化率比常规网站结账低三倍。该结果提醒零售商在引入对话式AI时，需要显式优化购物任务的流畅性，避免引入额外的操作摩擦。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gzw1987-bit.github.io/iching-math/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: The King Wen Permutation: [52, 10, 2]</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：王文卦置换：[52, 10, 2]</span></a><span class="dom">gzw1987-bit.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN presents a mathematical exploration of the King Wen permutation, using a simple triple [52, 10, 2] to illustrate a particular permutation in the I Ching framework.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了王文卦置换的数学探讨，以简单的三元组 [52, 10, 2] 为例来揭示在易经框架下的特定排列。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN presents a mathematical exploration of the King Wen permutation, using a simple triple [52, 10, 2] to illustrate a particular permutation in the I Ching framework. The project combines number theory with symbolic harmony, inviting readers to experiment with small seeds and observe emergent structure.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了王文卦置换的数学探讨，以简单的三元组 [52, 10, 2] 为例来揭示在易经框架下的特定排列。该项目把数论与象征意涵相结合，鼓励读者用小的种子进行实验，观察其中的结构与规律。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The article compares data manipulation ecosystems, highlighting Clojure&#x27;s approach with functional pipelines versus R&#x27;s data frames and Python&#x27;s pandas/polars.</span><span class='zh'>文章比较了数据处理生态：Clojure 的函数式流水线方法与 R 的数据框和 Python 的 pandas/polars 的差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The article compares data manipulation ecosystems, highlighting Clojure&#x27;s approach with functional pipelines versus R&#x27;s data frames and Python&#x27;s pandas/polars. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://lustin.fr/blog/building-digs/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Digs: iOS app that syncs your Discogs collection and lets you browse it offline</span><span class='zh'>Digs：离线浏览 Discogs 收藏的 iOS 应用</span></a><span class="dom">lustin.fr</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Digs provides offline access to your Discogs collection by syncing to an iOS app, enabling fast, private catalog browsing without a network.</span><span class='zh'>Digs 是一款 iOS 应用，能将 Discogs 收藏同步并离线浏览，便于本地快速、私密地管理音乐藏品。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Digs provides offline access to your Discogs collection by syncing to an iOS app, enabling fast, private catalog browsing without a network.</span><span class='zh'>Digs 是一款 iOS 应用，能将 Discogs 收藏同步并离线浏览，便于本地快速、私密地管理音乐藏品。</span></p>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Scott Hanselman teased work on Windows local accounts, hinting at reducing dependence on Microsoft accounts for sign-in and personalization. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://iev.ee/blog/the-quadratic-problem-nobody-fixed/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches has always been O(n²)</span><span class='zh'>寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度一直是 O(n²)</span></a><span class="dom">iev.ee</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches can deteriorate to quadratic time in worst cases, a long-standing performance pitfall in many engines.</span><span class='zh'>在最坏情况下，寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度可能退化为平方级。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Finding all regex matches can deteriorate to quadratic time in worst cases, a long-standing performance pitfall in many engines. The post outlines why this problem persists and offers practical approaches to mitigate it in real-world use.</span><span class='zh'>在最坏情况下，寻找所有正则匹配的时间复杂度可能退化为平方级。本文解释了这一问题为何长期未被根本解决，并给出在实际场景中缓解的可操作方法。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.da.vidbuchanan.co.uk/blog/dram-emfi.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Can you get root with only a cigarette lighter? (2024)</span><span class='zh'>只用打火机就能拿到 root 吗？（2024）</span></a><span class="dom">da.vidbuchanan.co.uk</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Researchers revisit a surprising hardware attack vector and show that a common cigarette lighter can act as a crude fault-injection tool to gain root access on a device.</span><span class='zh'>研究者重新探讨了一个出人意料的硬件攻击路径，展示了常见打火机也可作为粗糙的故障注入工具，从而在某些设备上获得 root 权限。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Researchers revisit a surprising hardware attack vector and show that a common cigarette lighter can act as a crude fault-injection tool to gain root access on a device. The write-up highlights how small, inexpensive hardware can create power transients or other disturbances that undermine security, underscoring the ongoing risk to embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>研究者重新探讨了一个出人意料的硬件攻击路径，展示了常见打火机也可作为粗糙的故障注入工具，从而在某些设备上获得 root 权限。该文章强调廉价小型硬件也会造成电源瞬变等干扰，暴露嵌入式系统安全的脆弱性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An in-depth look at the Bao I/O coprocessor, a tiny accelerator designed to offload I/O tasks and increase efficiency in embedded systems.</span><span class='zh'>对 Bao I/O 协处理器的深入解读，介绍其用于分担 I/O 任务、提升嵌入式系统性能与安全的设计思路。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An in-depth look at the Bao I/O coprocessor, a tiny accelerator designed to offload I/O tasks and increase efficiency in embedded systems. It examines architecture choices, potential uses, and what such a dedicated co-processor could mean for hobbyist and professional hardware.</span><span class='zh'>对 Bao I/O 协处理器的深入解读，介绍其用于分担 I/O 任务、提升嵌入式系统性能与安全的设计思路。文章探讨架构选型、潜在应用，以及这类专用协处理器对硬件爱好者与专业领域的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/antithesishq/bombadil" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Bombadil: Property-based testing for web UIs</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil：Antithesis 的网页 UI 属性测试工具</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Bombadil brings property-based testing to front-end UI testing, generating diverse interaction sequences to expose edge cases that example tests miss. It emphasizes automated shrinking of failing runs to minimal reproducing cases and integrates with common web stacks, helping teams catch flaky behavior before release.</span><span class='zh'>Bombadil 为前端 UI 引入属性测试，通过自动生成各种交互序列来揭示常规测试难以覆盖的边界问题。它还能对失败用例进行最小化缩减，便于定位复现，并与主流前端栈对接，帮助团队在发布前发现易出错的行为。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://emerge-lab.github.io/papers/an-unsolicited-guide-to-good-research.pdf" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An unsolicited guide to being a researcher [pdf]</span><span class='zh'>成为研究者的随手指南（PDF）</span></a><span class="dom">emerge-lab.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An Unsolicited Guide to Being a Researcher offers practical, no-nonsense advice on conducting robust, ethical research, from formulating questions to documenting methods and sharing results.</span><span class='zh'>这份《成为研究者的随手指南》给出务实、无废话的研究建议，覆盖从如何提出研究问题到记录方法、分享结果的全过程。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An Unsolicited Guide to Being a Researcher offers practical, no-nonsense advice on conducting robust, ethical research, from formulating questions to documenting methods and sharing results. It emphasizes reproducibility, critical thinking, and the social dynamics of collaboration for lasting impact.</span><span class='zh'>这份《成为研究者的随手指南》给出务实、无废话的研究建议，覆盖从如何提出研究问题到记录方法、分享结果的全过程。强调可重复性、批判性思维，以及在协作和学术共同体中的伦理与责任，帮助研究者提升长期影响力。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/EverMind-AI/MSA" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>MSA: Memory Sparse Attention</span><span class='zh'>MSA：记忆稀疏注意力</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>MSA introduces memory-sparse attention to reduce memory and compute for long-context models, enabling scalable attention with less quadratic cost. This can impact how we design and train larger sequences.</span><span class='zh'>MSA 提出记忆稀疏注意力，降低长上下文模型的内存与计算需求，使注意力计算更具可扩展性，或促使更大上下文的训练与推理。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.crowdsupply.com/baochip/dabao/updates/bio-the-bao-i-o-co-processor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>BIO – The Bao I/O Co-Processor</span><span class='zh'>BIO：Bao I/O 协处理器</span></a><span class="dom">crowdsupply.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Crowd Supply updates introduce BIO, a dedicated I/O co-processor for Bao hardware that handles GPIO and peripheral interfacing, offloading work from the main microcontroller.</span><span class='zh'>Crowd Supply 的更新介绍了 BIO（Bao I/O 协处理器），它作为 Bao 硬件的专用 I/O 协处理器，承担 GPIO 及外设接口的处理，减轻主控芯片的负担。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Crowd Supply updates introduce BIO, a dedicated I/O co-processor for Bao hardware that handles GPIO and peripheral interfacing, offloading work from the main microcontroller. The design aims to boost performance, simplify firmware, and enable more deterministic timing for embedded projects, with potential power and responsiveness benefits.</span><span class='zh'>Crowd Supply 的更新介绍了 BIO（Bao I/O 协处理器），它作为 Bao 硬件的专用 I/O 协处理器，承担 GPIO 及外设接口的处理，减轻主控芯片的负担。此设计旨在提升性能、简化固件并实现更确定性的时序，对嵌入式项目有潜在的功耗和响应优势。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">112</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://worksinprogress.co/issue/how-the-worlds-first-electric-grid-was-built/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>How the world’s first electric grid was built</span><span class='zh'>世界上第一座电网是如何建成的</span></a><span class="dom">worksinprogress.co</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This piece traces the birth of the world’s first electric grid, from isolated generators to a interconnected network.</span><span class='zh'>本文讲述世界上第一座电网的建立过程，揭示了从单一发电厂到城市级网络的技术与制度挑战。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This piece traces the birth of the world’s first electric grid, from isolated generators to a interconnected network. It explains early engineering trade-offs around generation methods, reliability, and business models that shaped the nascent electrical industry—and why those early decisions still influence grid modernization today.</span><span class='zh'>本文讲述世界上第一座电网的建立过程，揭示了从单一发电厂到城市级网络的技术与制度挑战。早期在发电方式、网路可靠性和商业模式等方面的权衡，至今仍影响着现代电网的改造与升级。</span></p>

<a class="thumb" href="https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/tony-hoare-and-his-imprint-on-computer-science/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><div class='th fb' style='--c:#10b981' data-tag='Science'><img class='fbi' src='https://www.google.com/s2/favicons?domain=cacm.acm.org&amp;sz=64' alt='' loading='lazy'><span class='fbd'>cacm.acm.org</span></div></a><button class="fav" aria-label="favorite">☆</button><button class="shr" aria-label="share" title="Share"><svg class='ico' viewBox='0 0 24 24'><path d='M12 3v12M7 8l5-5 5 5'/><path d='M5 13v6a2 2 0 0 0 2 2h10a2 2 0 0 0 2-2v-6'/></svg></button>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://cacm.acm.org/blogcacm/tony-hoare-and-his-imprint-on-computer-science/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Tony Hoare and His Imprint on Computer Science</span><span class='zh'>托尼·霍尔对计算机科学的影响</span></a><span class="dom">cacm.acm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece surveys Tony Hoare&#x27;s lasting impact on CS, from formal methods like Hoare logic to concurrent programming via CSP and algorithms such as quicksort.</span><span class='zh'>文章梳理了托尼·霍尔对计算机科学的深远影响，包括霍尔逻辑、并发编程的 CSP 以及快速排序等算法。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece surveys Tony Hoare&#x27;s lasting impact on CS, from formal methods like Hoare logic to concurrent programming via CSP and algorithms such as quicksort. It connects his ideas to modern software correctness, verification, and language design, illustrating how foundational theories shape practical systems.</span><span class='zh'>文章梳理了托尼·霍尔对计算机科学的深远影响，包括霍尔逻辑、并发编程的 CSP 以及快速排序等算法。将他的思想与现代软件正确性、验证和语言设计联系起来，展示基础理论如何塑造实际系统。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dfarq.homeip.net/whatever-happened-to-gem/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What happened to GEM?</span><span class='zh'>GEM 到底发生了什么？</span></a><span class="dom">dfarq.homeip.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>GEM, once a notable project/tool, faded from prominence; the piece investigates its rise and decline, drawing lessons on sustainability, community support, and tooling market dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>GEM 曾是备受关注的项目/工具，现已不再流行。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>GEM, once a notable project/tool, faded from prominence; the piece investigates its rise and decline, drawing lessons on sustainability, community support, and tooling market dynamics.</span><span class='zh'>GEM 曾是备受关注的项目/工具，现已不再流行。文章分析其兴衰，提出在社区支持、商业模式与市场需求方面的教训。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.scientificamerican.com/article/gerd-faltings-mathematician-who-proved-the-mordell-conjecture-wins-the-abel/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Gerd Faltings, who proved the Mordell conjecture, wins the Abel Prize</span><span class='zh'>格尔德·法尔汀斯因证明莫德尔猜想荣获 Abel 奖</span></a><span class="dom">scientificamerican.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Gerd Faltings solved the Mordell conjecture (Faltings&#x27; theorem), a milestone in number theory.</span><span class='zh'>格尔德·法尔汀斯因证明莫德尔猜想而获得 Abel 奖，这是数论领域的里程碑。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Gerd Faltings solved the Mordell conjecture (Faltings&#x27; theorem), a milestone in number theory. The Abel Prize honors this landmark contribution, underscoring its lasting impact on arithmetic geometry and our understanding of rational points on curves.</span><span class='zh'>格尔德·法尔汀斯因证明莫德尔猜想而获得 Abel 奖，这是数论领域的里程碑。法尔汀斯的定理对理解有理点在高阶代数曲线上的分布具有深远影响，被公认为算术几何的重要里程碑。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.nytimes.com/2026/03/21/business/media/cbs-news-radio-appraisal.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>An Aural Companion for Decades, CBS News Radio Crackles to a Close</span><span class='zh'>几十年的听觉陪伴，CBS 新闻广播走向终章</span></a><span class="dom">nytimes.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>After decades as a staple in US news radio, CBS News Radio is entering a new phase as its app winds down, reflecting a broader shift in how audiences consume audio news.</span><span class='zh'>CBS 新闻广播作为长期的新闻收听渠道，即将进入新阶段，相关应用逐步下线，凸显观众获取音频新闻方式在流媒体时代的转变。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>After decades as a staple in US news radio, CBS News Radio is entering a new phase as its app winds down, reflecting a broader shift in how audiences consume audio news. The shift underscores the fragility of long-running radio platforms in the streaming era.</span><span class='zh'>CBS 新闻广播作为长期的新闻收听渠道，即将进入新阶段，相关应用逐步下线，凸显观众获取音频新闻方式在流媒体时代的转变。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://martinfowler.com/articles/distributed-objects-microservices.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Microservices and the First Law of Distributed Objects (2014)</span><span class='zh'>微服务与分布式对象的第一定律（2014）</span></a><span class="dom">martinfowler.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler revisits the relationship between microservices and distributed objects, stressing explicit boundaries and asynchronous communication over naive remote method calls.</span><span class='zh'>Martin Fowler 将分布式对象的思想与微服务联系起来，强调应以明确边界和异步通信来避免远程方法调用的陷阱。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Martin Fowler revisits the relationship between microservices and distributed objects, stressing explicit boundaries and asynchronous communication over naive remote method calls.</span><span class='zh'>Martin Fowler 将分布式对象的思想与微服务联系起来，强调应以明确边界和异步通信来避免远程方法调用的陷阱。文章指出延迟、部分失败等问题，以及在规模化系统中维持清晰契约的重要性。</span></p>
